Continuing work from thread T-019b3664-f5b5-7006-824b-db413f42a0be. When you lack specific information you can use read_thread to get it.

@docs/feature/unified-text-editing-final.md @src/editable/mod.rs @src/editable/messages.rs @src/editable/context.rs @src/editable/state.rs @src/messages.rs @src/update/mod.rs @src/model/ui.rs @src/csv/model.rs @src/update/ui.rs @src/update/csv.rs

I'm implementing a unified text editing system across all input areas in the editor. The comprehensive plan is at docs/feature/unified-text-editing-final.md (~3,700 lines).

I just completed Phase 1 - creating the src/editable/ module foundation:
- TextBuffer/TextBufferMut traits abstracting over Rope and String backends
- StringBuffer and RopeBuffer implementations
- Position, Cursor, Selection structs using line/column (not byte offsets)
- EditableState<B> as the main abstraction with all editing operations (movement, selection, insert, delete, word operations, undo/redo)
- EditConstraints for context-specific restrictions (single-line, numeric filter, max length, etc.)
- EditHistory for undo/redo
- TextEditMsg enum with MoveTarget for cursor movement
- EditContext enum for routing (Editor, CommandPalette, GotoLine, FindQuery, ReplaceQuery, CsvCell)
- 52 unit tests all passing, clippy clean

Phase 2 requires:
- Add Msg::TextEdit(EditContext, TextEditMsg) variant to src/messages.rs
- Create src/update/text_edit.rs with update_text_edit() unified handler
- Wire up dispatch in src/update/mod.rs to route TextEdit messages
- The plan specifies the handler should match on context to find the appropriate EditableState and apply the message

Key patterns:
- Use from_text() not from_str() for buffer constructors (clippy requirement)
- Word boundary detection uses existing CharType from src/util/text.rs
- Cursor uses line/column to match existing EditorState model
- EditContext::constraints() returns appropriate EditConstraints for each context

The plan includes complete Rust implementations for all types - reference docs/feature/unified-text-editing-final.md sections 5.1-5.3 for Phase 2 details.

proceed to phase 2

## Assistant

**Phase 2 Complete!**

Summary of changes:

1. **src/messages.rs**: Added `Msg::TextEdit(EditContext, TextEditMsg)` variant with import from `crate::editable`

2. **src/update/text_edit.rs** (new file): Created with:
   - `update_text_edit()` - Routes TextEditMsg to appropriate context (placeholder stubs for Phase 4)
   - `apply_text_edit_msg()` - Applies a TextEditMsg to an EditableState<StringBuffer>
   - `apply_move()` - Generic movement helper
   - 6 unit tests

3. **src/update/mod.rs**:
   - Added `mod text_edit`
   - Exported `apply_text_edit_msg` and `update_text_edit`
   - Added dispatch for `Msg::TextEdit` in `update_inner()`
   - Added `TextEdit` case to debug `msg_type_name()`

**Test results**: 253 tests passing, clippy clean

The unified message infrastructure is now in place. Phase 3 (rendering consolidation) and Phase 4 (migration of modal states to use EditableState) can now proceed.

The alpha value from the\n14: /// foreground color determines the blend ratio.\n15: ///\n16: /// Returns the blended color with full opacity (alpha = 0xFF).\n17: #[inline]\n18: pub fn blend_colors(bg: u32, fg: u32, alpha: f32) -> u32 {\n19:     let bg_r = ((bg >> 16) & 0xFF) as f32;\n20:     let bg_g = ((bg >> 8) & 0xFF) as f32;\n21:     let bg_b = (bg & 0xFF) as f32;\n22: \n23:     let fg_r = ((fg >> 16) & 0xFF) as f32;\n24:     let fg_g = ((fg >> 8) & 0xFF) as f32;\n25:     let fg_b = (fg & 0xFF) as f32;\n26: \n27:     let final_r = (bg_r * (1.0 - alpha) + fg_r * alpha) as u32;\n28:     let final_g = (bg_g * (1.0 - alpha) + fg_g * alpha) as u32;\n29:     let final_b = (bg_b * (1.0 - alpha) + fg_b * alpha) as u32;\n30: \n31:     0xFF000000 | (final_r << 16) | (final_g << 8) | final_b\n32: }\n33: \n34: /// A frame buffer wrapper providing safe drawing primitives.\n35: ///\n36: /// All coordinates are in pixels. Out-of-bounds operations are safely clipped.\n37: pub struct Frame<'a> {\n38:     buffer: &'a mut [u32],\n39:     width: usize,\n40:     height: usize,\n41: }\n42: \n43: impl<'a> Frame<'a> {\n44:     /// Create a new frame from a mutable pixel buffer\n45:     ///\n46:     /// If the buffer is smaller than width*height, dimensions are adjusted\n47:     /// to match the actual buffer size to prevent out-of-bounds access.\n48:     pub fn new(buffer: &'a mut [u32], width: usize, height: usize) -> Self {\n49:         let expected_size = width * height;\n50:         let actual_size = buffer.len();\n51: \n52:         let (width, height) = if actual_size < expected_size && width > 0 {\n53:             // Buffer is smaller than expected - super::frame::Frame;\nuse super::TextPainter;\n\n/// Options for rendering a text field.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct TextFieldOptions {\n    /// X position of text area in pixels\n    pub x: usize,\n    /// Y position of text area in pixels\n    pub y: usize,\n    /// Width of text area in pixels\n    pub width: usize,\n    /// Height of text area in pixels (typically line_height)\n cursor_visible: bool,\n    /// Horizontal scroll offset in characters\n    pub scroll_x: usize,\n}\n\nimpl Default for TextFieldOptions {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            x: 0,\n            y: 0,\n            width: 200,\n            height: 20,\n            char_width: 8.0,\n            line_height: 20,\n            text_color: 0xFFFFFFFF,\n            cursor_color: 0xFFFFFFFF,\n            selection_color: 0x40FFFFFF,\n            cursor_visible: true,\n            scroll_x: 0,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Trait for content that can be rendered as a text field.\n///\n/// This allows uniform rendering of different editable states.\npub trait TextFieldContent {\n    /// Get the text content for line 0 (single-line inputs)\n    fn text(&self) -> &str;\n\n    /// Get all cursors\n    fn cursors(&self) -> &[Cursor];\n\n    /// Get all selections\n    fn selections(&self) -> &[Selection];\n\n    /// Get the active cursor index\n    fn active_cursor_index(&self) -> usize;\n}\n\nimpl TextFieldContent for EditableState<StringBuffer> {\n    fn text(&self) -> &str {\n        self.buffer.as_str()\n    }\n\n    fn cursors(&self) -> &[Cursor] {\n        &self.cursors\n    }\n\n    fn selections(&self) -> &[Selection] {\n        &self.selections\n    }\n\n    fn active_cursor_index(&self) -> usize {\n        self.active_cursor\n    }\n}\n\n/// Unified renderer for text fields.\n///\n/// Handles rendering of:\n/// - Selection backgrounds\n/// - Text content\n/// - Cursor(s)\npub struct TextFieldRenderer;\n\nimpl TextFieldRenderer {\n    /// Render a text field from an EditableState.\n    pub fn render(\n        frame: &mut Frame,\n        painter: &mut TextPainter,\n        content: &dyn TextFieldContent,\n        opts: &TextFieldOptions,\n    ) {\n        let text = content.text();\n\n        // 1. Render selection backgrounds\n        for selection in content.selections() {\n            if selection.is_empty() {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // For single-line, we only care about column positions\n            let start_col = selection.start().column;\n            let end_col = selection.end().column;\n\n            // Adjust for horizontal scroll\n            let visible_start = start_col.saturating_sub(opts.scroll_x);\n            let visible_end = end_col.saturating_sub(opts.scroll_x);\n\n            if visible_end > visible_start {\n                let sel_x = opts.x + (visible_start as f32 * opts.char_width).round() as usize;\n                let sel_width =\n                    ((visible_end - visible_start) as f32 * opts.char_width).round() as usize;\n\n                // Clamp to visible width\n                let clamped_width = sel_width.min(opts.width.saturating_sub(sel_x - opts.x));\n\n                if clamped_width > 0 {\n                    frame.fill_rect_px(sel_x, opts.y, clamped_width, opts.height, opts.selection_color);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // 2. Render text (with horizontal scroll)\n        let max_chars = (opts.width as f32 / opts.char_width).ceil() as usize + 1;\n        let visible_text: String = text\n            .chars()\n            .skip(opts.scroll_x)\n            .take(max_chars)\n            .collect();\n\n        painter.draw(frame, opts.x, opts.y, &visible_text, opts.text_color);\n\n        // 3. Render cursors\n        if opts.cursor_visible {\n            for (idx, cursor) in content.cursors().iter().enumerate() {\n                let col = cursor.column.saturating_sub(opts.scroll_x);\n                let cursor_x = opts.x + (col as f32 * opts.char_width).round() as usize;\n\n                // Check if cursor is visible in viewport\n                if cursor_x >= opts.x && cursor_x < opts.x + opts.width {\n                    let color = if idx == content.active_cursor_index() {\n                        opts.cursor_color\n                    } else {\n                        // Slightly dimmer for secondary cursors\n                        opts.cursor_color & 0x80FFFFFF\n                    };\n\n                    // 2px wide cursor bar\n                    frame.fill_rect_px(\n                        cursor_x,\n                        opts.y + 1,\n                        2,\n                        opts.height.saturating_sub(2),\n                        color,\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Render a simple text field from raw text and cursor position.\n    ///\n    /// This is a convenience method for cases where we don't have a full\n    /// EditableState yet (during migration).\n    pub fn render_simple(\n        frame: &mut Frame,\n        painter: &mut TextPainter,\n        text: &str,\n        cursor_col: usize,\n        opts: &TextFieldOptions,\n    ) {\n        // Render text\n        let max_chars = (opts.width as f32 / opts.char_width).ceil() as usize + 1;\n        let visible_text: String = text\n            .chars()\n            .skip(opts.scroll_x)\n            .take(max_chars)\n            .collect();\n\n        painter.draw(frame, opts.x, opts.y, &visible_text, opts.text_color);\n\n        // Render cursor\n        if opts.cursor_visible {\n            let col = cursor_col.saturating_sub(opts.scroll_x);\n            let cursor_x = opts.x + (col as f32 * opts.char_width).round() as usize;\n\n            if cursor_x >= opts.x && cursor_x < opts.x + opts.width {\n                frame.fill_rect_px(\n                    cursor_x,\n                    opts.y + 1,\n                    2,\n                    opts.height.saturating_sub(2),\n                    opts.cursor_color,\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Calculate the scroll offset needed to keep the cursor visible.\n    ///\n    /// Returns the new scroll_x value.\n    pub fn calculate_scroll(cursor_col: usize, scroll_x: usize, visible_chars: usize) -> usize {\n        // Keep some margin around the cursor\n        let margin = 2;\n\n        if cursor_col < scroll_x + margin {\n            // Cursor is too far left, scroll left\n            cursor_col.saturating_sub(margin)\n        } else if cursor_col >= scroll_x + visible_chars.saturating_sub(margin) {\n            // Cursor is too far right, scroll right\n            cursor_col.saturating_sub(visible_chars.saturating_sub(margin + 1))\n        } else {\n            // Cursor is visible, no change\n            scroll_x\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Simple wrapper for rendering text fields during migration.\n///\n/// Holds text and cursor position without full EditableState.\npub struct SimpleTextField {\n    text: String,\n    cursor: Cursor,\n    selection: Selection,\n}\n\nimpl SimpleTextField {\n    pub fn new(text: &str) -> Self {\n        let cursor = Cursor::new(0, text.chars().count());\n        Self {\n            text: text.to_string(),\n            cursor,\n            selection: Selection::collapsed(Position::new(0, cursor.column)),\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn with_cursor(text: &str, cursor_col: usize) -> Self {\n        let cursor = Cursor::new(0, cursor_col);\n        Self {\n            text: text.to_string(),\n            cursor,\n            selection: Selection::collapsed(Position::new(0, cursor_col)),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl TextFieldContent for SimpleTextField {\n    fn text(&self) -> &str {\n        &self.text\n    }\n\n    fn cursors(&self) -> &[Cursor] {\n        std::slice::from_ref(&self.cursor)\n    }\n\n    fn selections(&self) -> &[Selection] {\n        std::slice::from_ref(&self.selection)\n    }\n\n    fn active_cursor_index(&self) -> usize {\n        0\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_calculate_scroll_cursor_visible() {\n        // Cursor at column 5, scroll at 0, 20 chars visible\n        assert_eq!(TextFieldRenderer::calculate_scroll(5, 0, 20), 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_calculate_scroll_cursor_too_far_right() {\n        // Cursor at column 25, scroll at 0, 20 chars visible\n        let scroll = TextFieldRenderer::calculate_scroll(25, 0, 20);\n        assert!(scroll > 0);\n        // Cursor should be visible with margin\n        assert!(25 >= scroll && 25 < scroll + 20);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_calculate_scroll_cursor_too_far_left() {\n        // Cursor at column 5, scroll at 10, 20 chars visible\n        let scroll = TextFieldRenderer::calculate_scroll(5, 10, 20);\n        assert!(scroll <= 5);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_simple_text_field() {\n        let field = SimpleTextField::new(\"hello\");\n        assert_eq!(field.text(), \"hello\");\n        assert_eq!(field.cursors().len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(field.cursors()[0].column, 5); // cursor at end\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_simple_text_field_with_cursor() {\n        let field = SimpleTextField::with_cursor(\"hello\", 2);\n        assert_eq!(field.cursors()[0].column, 2);\n    }\n}\n"
